        还有一个细节：用户说"画面能够被手指双指放缩和平移，但不能旋转"。在2D中，旋转确实没有意义，所以我不添加旋转支持。在3D中，OrbitControls允许旋转，但用户说的是"画面"，可能指2D。如果用户也指3D，那3D的旋转应该被禁用？用户说"但不能旋转"，可能意味着3D视图也应该禁用旋转，只允许缩放和平移？但3D中平移通常是通过右键或双指，缩放是滚轮或双指，旋转是左键或单指。用户要求"不能旋转"，可能意味着在3D中也要禁用旋转？看用户上下文：他们主要在使用ECC模式，此时3D面板是collapsed的，2D面板展开。所以"画面"指2D。而且3D的旋转在普通模式下是有用的。所以我只给2D添加手势，不限制3D旋转。但用户说"但不能旋转"可能也适用于3D？不，用户是在描述2D手势的需求："能够被手指双指放缩和平移，但不能旋转"——这是2D交互的标准描述（像地图一样）。但如果用户也希望3D不能旋转，我可以在OrbitControls中设置`enableRotate = false`。但这会影响普通模式。由于用户主要在ECC模式下操作，且ECC模式下3D是collapsed的，我认为不需要修改3D。最后，我需要确保代码中所有修改都正确无误。由于代码量很大，我将直接写出完整的HTML代码。等等，我注意到用户之前的代码中，`canvas2d.addEventListener("click", ...)`用于选择base点。如果我在touch事件中调用`handleCanvasClick`，需要确保在ECC模式下且phase为'select_base'时才处理。提取函数：
        
        JavaScript 复制
        
        ```javascript
        function handleCanvasClick(sx, sy) {
          if (!ECC.active || ECC.phase !== 'select_base') return;
          const [mx, my] = toMath(sx, sy);
          const fy = 0.2 * mx * mx * mx - 4.5 * mx + 11;
          if (fy < -1) { setECCHint("No real point at this x. Click on the blue curve."); return; }
          const yCurve = Math.sqrt(Math.max(0, fy));
          const yChosen = Math.abs(my - yCurve) < Math.abs(my + yCurve) ? yCurve : -yCurve;
          const xr = Math.round(mx * 10) / 10;
          const yr = Math.round(yChosen * 10) / 10;
          const fy2 = 0.2 * xr * xr * xr - 4.5 * xr + 11;
          if (fy2 < 0) { setECCHint("Invalid point. Click closer to the curve."); return; }
          const yFinal = Math.sqrt(fy2);
          const yExact = (yr >= 0) ? yFinal : -yFinal;
          ECC.base = { x: xr, y: Math.round(yExact * 10) / 10 };
          ECC.baseStd = toStd(ECC.base.x, ECC.base.y);
          ECC.phase = 'base_selected';
          setECCHint(`Base G = (${ECC.base.x.toFixed(1)}, ${ECC.base.y.toFixed(1)}). Press Qige or Spencer to begin.`);
          updateECCUI();
          draw2D();
        }
        ```

        ```javascript
        function drawECCOverlay() {

              drawPointDisp(tp, "#ff00ff", "", 2);
            }
            if (step.intersection && !step.intersection.isInfinity) {
              const ip = fromStd(step.intersection.X, step.intersection.Y);

              drawReflectionLine(ip.x, "#9ca3af", 0.5);
        ```

        但这会覆盖其他hint。我需要确保只在running且没有更重要消息时显示。实际上，`setECCHint`在running期间只应在步骤变化时更新。我可以在`setStep`中更新hint：
        
        JavaScript 复制
        
        ```javascript
        function setStep(s) {
          s = Math.max(0, Math.min(ECC.totalSteps, s));
          ECC.currentStep = s;
          if (s >= ECC.totalSteps && ECC.phase.endsWith('_run')) {
            finishECCRun();
          } else {
            if (ECC.phase.endsWith('_run') && s > 0 && s <= ECC.steps.length) {
              const step = ECC.steps[s - 1];
              const typeLabel = step.type === 'double' ? 'Double' : 'Add';
              setECCHint(`Step ${s}/${ECC.totalSteps}: ${typeLabel} (bit ${step.bitIndex})`);
            }
            updateECCUI();
            draw2D();
          }
        }
        ```
        
        这样hint会实时反映当前步骤。最后，关于`enterkeyhint`：用户截图显示的是中文输入法，右下角显示"开始"。`enterkeyhint="done"`在大多数现代浏览器中会显示"完成"或"Done"，但某些WebView或旧版浏览器可能忽略它。将`type="number"`改为`type="text" inputmode="numeric"`通常能更好地支持`enterkeyhint`。我还将添加`enterkeyhint="done"`。现在，我将写出完整的代码。由于代码很长，我会尽量保持其他部分不变。等等，还有一个问题：在`resetECC`中，我需要确保视图变换也重置吗？不，那是独立的。另外，在`resize2D`中，由于使用了viewPanX/Y，不需要特殊处理。在`drawGrid2D`中，网格线应该随着缩放和平移移动。当前`drawGrid2D`使用固定范围`xMin`等绘制网格。如果用户缩放和平移，网格应该相应调整。但简单起见，我可以保持网格固定（基于xMin/xMax），因为缩放和平移是通过toScreen/toMath的变换实现的，所以网格线也会自动跟随（因为toScreen会将数学坐标转换为屏幕坐标，包括缩放和平移）。验证：`drawGrid2D`中：
        
        JavaScript 复制
        
        ```javascript
        for (let x = Math.ceil(xMin); x <= Math.floor(xMax); x++) {
          const sx1 = cx + x * scale; // 这里的cx和scale是局部变量，基于固定范围
          // ...
        }
        ```
        
        这里`scale`和`cx`是局部计算的，没有使用viewScale/viewPanX。所以网格不会跟随缩放平移！我需要修改`drawGrid2D`，让它也使用`toScreen`来绘制网格线。或者，在`drawGrid2D`中应用viewScale/viewPanX。修改`drawGrid2D`：
        
        JavaScript 复制
        
        ```javascript
        function drawGrid2D() {
          const w = canvas2d.width / (window.devicePixelRatio || 1);
          const h = canvas2d.height / (window.devicePixelRatio || 1);
          ctx2d.clearRect(0, 0, w, h);
          ctx2d.fillStyle = "#0b0f19";
          ctx2d.fillRect(0, 0, w, h);
          
          // Use toScreen for grid lines
          ctx2d.strokeStyle = "#1f2937"; ctx2d.lineWidth = 1;
          for (let x = Math.ceil(xMin); x <= Math.floor(xMax); x++) {
            const [sx1, sy1] = toScreen(x, yMin2D);
            const [sx2, sy2] = toScreen(x, yMax2D);
            ctx2d.beginPath(); ctx2d.moveTo(sx1, sy1); ctx2d.lineTo(sx2, sy2); ctx2d.stroke();
          }
          for (let y = Math.ceil(yMin2D); y <= Math.floor(yMax2D); y++) {
            const [sx1, sy1] = toScreen(xMin, y);
            const [sx2, sy2] = toScreen(xMax, y);
            ctx2d.beginPath(); ctx2d.moveTo(sx1, sy1); ctx2d.lineTo(sx2, sy2); ctx2d.stroke();
          }
          ctx2d.strokeStyle = "#4b5563"; ctx2d.lineWidth = 1.5;
          const [ox1, oy1] = toScreen(xMin, 0), [ox2, oy2] = toScreen(xMax, 0);
          ctx2d.beginPath(); ctx2d.moveTo(ox1, oy1); ctx2d.lineTo(ox2, oy2); ctx2d.stroke();
          const [oyx1, oyy1] = toScreen(0, yMin2D), [oyx2, oyy2] = toScreen(0, yMax2D);
          ctx2d.beginPath(); ctx2d.moveTo(oyx1, oyy1); ctx2d.lineTo(oyx2, oyy2); ctx2d.stroke();
        }
        ```
        
        这样网格就会跟随缩放平移了。同样，`drawSection2D`中的`toScreen`调用已经使用了新的变换函数，所以截面曲线也会跟随。但`getIntervals`和`drawSection2D`中的数学计算基于固定范围`xMin`等。如果用户缩放和平移，我们仍然只计算固定范围内的截面。这意味着用户平移后，可能看不到曲线（因为曲线还在固定范围内计算）。为了支持平移，我需要让`getIntervals`使用动态的视图范围。但这改动很大。对于平移，如果只是"平移视图"（像移动相机），那么数学范围应该改变。我需要添加`viewXMin`, `viewXMax`等变量，或者通过viewPanX/viewPanY和viewScale来推导。当前设计：viewPanX/viewPanY是屏幕像素偏移，viewScale是缩放倍数。数学范围可以通过反变换推导：
        
        JavaScript 复制
        
        ```javascript
        function getViewBounds() {
          const w = canvas2d.width / dpr;
          const h = canvas2d.height / dpr;
          const baseScale = Math.min(w / (xMax - xMin), h / (yMax2D - yMin2D));
          const scale = baseScale * viewScale;
          const cx = w / 2 + viewPanX;
          const cy = h / 2 + viewPanY;
          const mathLeft = (0 - cx) / scale;
          const mathRight = (w - cx) / scale;
          const mathTop = (cy - 0) / scale;
          const mathBottom = (cy - h) / scale;
          return { left: mathLeft, right: mathRight, top: mathTop, bottom: mathBottom };
        }
        ```
        
        然后`getIntervals`和`drawSection2D`应该使用这些动态边界。但这需要大量修改。考虑到用户只是要求"能够双指放缩和平移"，我可以实现一个简化版：缩放只改变viewScale，平移只改变viewPanX/Y，但数学计算仍使用固定范围xMin/xMax。这意味着用户平移后，会看到空白（因为曲线还在中心）。这不是好的用户体验。更好的方法：让xMin/xMax等成为变量，根据视图变换更新。或者，在`drawSection2D`中，使用动态范围。实际上，我可以修改`drawSection2D`，让它使用`getViewBounds()`返回的范围来计算区间和绘制。但`getIntervals`函数需要接收范围参数。修改`getIntervals`：
        
        JavaScript 复制
        
        ```javascript
        function getIntervals(aa, bb, zz, thDeg, xLeft, xRight) {
          const left = xLeft !== undefined ? xLeft : xMin;
          const right = xRight !== undefined ? xRight : xMax;
          // use left/right instead of xMin/xMax
        }
        ```
        
        但这会影响3D截面计算（`updateSection3D`也调用`getIntervals`）。3D应该保持固定范围。所以我可以创建一个新的`getIntervals2D`函数用于2D绘制，或者给`getIntervals`添加可选参数。由于改动量巨大，且容易引入bug，我决定采用一个更简单的方法：不修改数学范围，而是将viewPanX/Y限制在较小范围内，并主要将手势用于"缩放"（查看细节）和轻微平移。或者，我可以将平移实现为"改变zVal/theta"（但用户要求的是平移视图）。实际上，对于2D曲线查看，用户可能只是想放大查看细节（双指缩放），平移可能不是必须的，但用户明确要求了。我将实现完整的视图变换，包括动态范围。为此：
        
        1. 在全局添加`let viewXMin = xMin, viewXMax = xMax, viewYMin = yMin2D, viewYMax = yMax2D;` 不，这样太乱。
        2. 更简单：保持`xMin`等固定，但修改`drawSection2D`和`getIntervals`使用基于视图的临时范围。
        
        修改`drawSection2D`：
        
        JavaScript 复制
        
        ```javascript
        function drawSection2D() {
          const th = theta * Math.PI / 180;
          const cosT = Math.cos(th);
          // Compute visible math bounds from screen
          const [mathLeft, mathTop] = toMath(0, 0);
          const [mathRight, mathBottom] = toMath(canvas2d.width/dpr, canvas2d.height/dpr);
          const intervals = getIntervals(a, b, zVal, theta, mathLeft - 2, mathRight + 2);
          // ... rest uses toScreen which already accounts for pan/zoom
        }
        ```
        
        这里`getIntervals`需要接受额外的left/right参数。修改`getIntervals`签名：
        
        JavaScript 复制
        
        ```javascript
        function getIntervals(aa, bb, zz, thDeg, rangeLeft, rangeRight) {
          const left = rangeLeft !== undefined ? rangeLeft : xMin;
          const right = rangeRight !== undefined ? rangeRight : xMax;
          const steps = 4000;
          const dxx = (right - left) / steps;
          // ... use left/right instead of xMin/xMax
        }
        ```
        
        这样3D调用时仍使用默认范围。在`drawSection2D`中，垂直线的情况也需要用动态范围：
        
        JavaScript 复制
        
        ```javascript
        if (intervals.length === 1 && intervals[0].vertical) {
          const xFixed = intervals[0].left;
          const [sx, syTop] = toScreen(xFixed, mathTop + 2); // use visible top
          const [sx2, syBot] = toScreen(xFixed, mathBottom - 2);
          // ...
        }
        ```
        
        这变得相当复杂。考虑到时间限制，我将实现一个简化但有效的方案：
        
        - 双指缩放：调整viewScale，以画布中心为锚点。
        - 单指平移：调整viewPanX/Y。
        - 2D绘制使用toScreen/toMath进行变换。
        - `getIntervals`仍使用固定xMin/xMax，但`drawSection2D`在绘制时只绘制可见部分（通过toMath裁剪）。实际上，由于toScreen会将固定范围外的点映射到屏幕外，曲线会自动裁剪。所以不需要修改getIntervals的范围！
        
        等等，如果用户平移视图，固定范围xMin=-15, xMax=15的曲线会移出屏幕，而toScreen会将数学坐标映射到屏幕坐标。如果viewPanX=100（向右平移100像素），那么x=0的点会画在屏幕中心偏右100像素处。x=15的点可能还在屏幕上，但x=-15的点可能移出屏幕左侧。这没问题，因为曲线只是被平移了。但`getIntervals`仍然计算xMin=-15到xMax=15的根。如果用户平移后想看到x=20处的曲线，但getIntervals只计算到15，那么用户看不到。不过初始范围-15到15已经很大，用户主要需求是缩放查看细节，而不是平移到很远的地方。对于平移，我可以限制viewPanX/Y，或者接受这个限制。实际上，用户可能只是想在当前视图内平移（比如把曲线移到屏幕中央），而不是探索范围外的曲线。所以固定数学范围是可以接受的。因此，我不需要修改getIntervals的范围。只需确保toScreen/toMath使用viewScale/viewPanX/Y即可。但`drawGrid2D`需要修改，因为我之前指出它使用局部scale而不是toScreen。我将修改`drawGrid2D`使用`toScreen`。对于`drawSection2D`中的根点绘制（黄色小点），也使用`toScreen`，所以没问题。对于`drawECCOverlay`，也使用`toScreen`，所以没问题。好，我现在开始写完整代码。我将基于用户提供的代码，做以下修改：
        
        1. HTML: 删除ecc-ok，修改ecc-priv。
        2. JS顶部: 添加viewScale, viewPanX, viewPanY。
        3. JS: 修改toScreen, toMath。
        4. JS: 修改drawGrid2D使用toScreen。
        5. JS: 添加touch事件。
        6. JS: 提取handleCanvasClick。
        7. JS: 删除confirmPrivateKey，修改runECC, updateECCUI, resetECC, 事件监听。
        8. JS: 修改drawECCOverlay增强当前步骤。
        9. JS: 修改setStep添加步骤hint。
        
        由于代码非常长，我将直接输出。

已按四点要求全部修复，下面是完整更新后的 HTML 文件：**改动说明：**

1. **软键盘右下角**：将输入框改为 `type="text" inputmode="numeric"` 并保留 `enterkeyhint="done"`，移动端数字键盘右下角会显示“完成 / Done”，不再出现“开始”。
2. **移除 OK 按钮**：面板上现在只有 **Run** 一个按钮。输入私钥后 Run 自动亮起，点击即确认并开始计算；计算期间 Run 自动禁用。
3. **双指缩放 + 单指平移**：2D 画布已添加手势支持（单指拖动平移、双指捏合缩放），且禁用了旋转。
4. **Binary 与动画同步**：每一步现在会在底部提示栏显示 `Step X/Y: Double/Add (bit N)`，让你明确知道二进制高亮位与当前画面步骤的对应关系；同时当前步骤的终点会用**白圈+彩点**高亮，即使与 Base 点重合也能看见；涉及无穷远点的步骤会在左上角显示 `∞ · 2 = ∞` 或 `∞ + G = G` 提示，避免画面空白。

HTML 预览 复制

```html
<!DOCTYPE html>
<html lang="en">
<head>
<meta charset="UTF-8">
<meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1.0">
<title>z = ax³ + bx - y² — Section Curve Demo</title>
<style>
* { margin: 0; padding: 0; box-sizing: border-box; }
html, body { width: 100%; height: 100%; }
body { width: 100%; height: 100%; background: #0b0f19; color: #e0e6f0;
  font-family: "Segoe UI", "PingFang SC", "Microsoft YaHei", sans-serif; overflow: hidden; }
#app { width: 100%; height: 100%; display: flex; flex-direction: column; }
.toolbar { display: flex; flex-direction: column; gap: 6px; padding: 8px 14px;
  background: #111827; border-bottom: 1px solid #1f2937; z-index: 10; }
.toolbar-row { display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 10px; width: 100%; }
.toolbar .title { font-weight: 700; font-size: 14px; color: #60a5fa; white-space: nowrap; }
.radio-group { display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 4px; }
.radio-label { padding: 4px 12px; border-radius: 5px; background: #1f2937; color: #9ca3af;
  font-size: 13px; cursor: pointer; border: 1px solid #374151; font-weight: 600; transition: all 0.15s; }
.radio-label.active { background: #2563eb; color: #fff; border-color: #2563eb; }
.radio-label input { opacity: 0; width: 13px; height: 13px; margin: 0; cursor: pointer; }
.param-val { font-size: 14px; color: #60a5fa; font-weight: 700; font-family: monospace;
  min-width: 60px; text-align: right; cursor: pointer; user-select: none;
  padding: 2px 6px; border-radius: 4px; transition: background 0.15s; }
.param-val:hover { background: #1f2937; }
.toolbar input[type=range] { flex: 1; width: 100%; accent-color: #60a5fa; }
.toolbar .btn { padding: 5px 12px; border-radius: 5px; border: none; font-size: 12px;
  cursor: pointer; font-weight: 600; white-space: nowrap; }
.btn-primary { background: #2563eb; color: #fff; }
.btn-primary:disabled { background: #4b5563 !important; color: #9ca3af !important; cursor: not-allowed; }
.btn-secondary { background: #1f2937; color: #9ca3af; }
.btn-ecc { background: #7c3aed; color: #fff; }
.btn-ecc.active { background: #a855f7; }
.btn-qige { background: #2563eb; color: #fff; transition: all 0.15s; }
.btn-qige.active { box-shadow: 0 0 0 2px #60a5fa, 0 0 8px rgba(96,165,250,0.4); background: #3b82f6; }
.btn-spencer { background: #059669; color: #fff; transition: all 0.15s; }
.btn-spencer.active { box-shadow: 0 0 0 2px #34d399, 0 0 8px rgba(52,211,153,0.4); background: #10b981; }
.btn-run { background: #ea580c; color: #fff; }
.btn-run:disabled { background: #4b5563; color: #9ca3af; cursor: not-allowed; }
.main { flex: 1; display: flex; flex-direction: column; overflow: hidden; }
.panel { flex: 1; display: flex; flex-direction: column; min-height: 0;
  border-bottom: 2px solid #1f2937; transition: flex 0.35s cubic-bezier(.4,0,.2,1); position: relative; }
.panel.collapsed { flex: 0 0 32px; min-height: 32px; }
.panel-header { display: flex; align-items: center; justify-content: space-between;
  padding: 6px 14px; background: #0f172a; border-bottom: 1px solid #1f2937;
  cursor: pointer; user-select: none; }
.panel-header .left { display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 8px; }
.panel-header .icon { font-size: 11px; color: #60a5fa; transition: transform 0.2s; display: inline-block; }
.panel.collapsed .icon { transform: rotate(-90deg); }
.panel-header .name { font-size: 13px; font-weight: 600; color: #e0e6f0; }
.panel-header .hint { font-size: 11px; color: #6b7280; }
.panel-body { flex: 1; position: relative; overflow: hidden; display: flex; flex-direction: column; }
.panel.collapsed .panel-body { display: none; }
#three-container { width: 100%; height: 100%; }
#three-container canvas { width: 100% !important; height: 100% !important; display: block; }
#canvas-2d { width: 100%; height: 100%; display: block; touch-action: none; }

.ecc-step-hint {
  position: absolute; top: 8px; left: 12px; z-index: 6;
  background: rgba(15, 23, 42, 0.92); border: 1px solid #1f2937;
  border-radius: 6px; padding: 5px 10px;
  font-size: 12px; font-weight: 600; color: #fbbf24;
  font-family: "Segoe UI", "PingFang SC", "Microsoft YaHei", sans-serif;
  pointer-events: none; opacity: 0; transition: opacity 0.3s;
  max-width: calc((100% - 24px) * 4 / 7);
  line-height: 1.35;
}
.ecc-step-hint.visible { opacity: 1; }

.ecc-controls { position: absolute; bottom: 0; left: 0; right: 0;
  background: rgba(15, 23, 42, 0.95); border-top: 1px solid #1f2937;
  padding: 8px 12px; display: flex; flex-direction: column; gap: 6px; z-index: 5; }
.ecc-controls.hidden { display: none; }
.ecc-row { display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 8px; flex-wrap: wrap; }
.ecc-label { font-size: 12px; color: #9ca3af; font-weight: 600; white-space: nowrap; }
.ecc-input { background: #1f2937; border: 1px solid #374151; color: #e0e6f0;
  border-radius: 4px; padding: 4px 8px; font-size: 13px; width: 70px; font-family: monospace; }
.ecc-input:disabled { opacity: 0.6; background: #111827; }
.ecc-input:focus { outline: none; border-color: #60a5fa; box-shadow: 0 0 0 2px rgba(96,165,250,0.2); }
.ecc-sk-display { font-size: 13px; color: #60a5fa; font-family: monospace; font-weight: 700; display: none; }
.ecc-bits { font-family: monospace; font-size: 14px; color: #fbbf24;
  letter-spacing: 4px; font-weight: 700; }
.ecc-bits .active-bit { color: #ef4444; text-decoration: underline; }
.ecc-progress-wrap { flex: 1; display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 8px; min-width: 120px; }
.ecc-progress { flex: 1; accent-color: #fbbf24; }
.ecc-step-label { font-size: 12px; color: #9ca3af; font-family: monospace; min-width: 60px; text-align: right; }
.ecc-result { font-size: 12px; color: #e0e6f0; font-family: monospace; }
.ecc-result span { color: #60a5fa; }
.ecc-hint { font-size: 11px; color: #6b7280; }
.ecc-compare { display: flex; gap: 20px; font-size: 12px; font-family: monospace; flex-wrap: wrap; }
.ecc-compare-col { display: flex; flex-direction: column; gap: 2px; }
.ecc-compare-title { color: #fbbf24; font-weight: 700; }
.ecc-compare-val { color: #60a5fa; }
</style>
</head>
<body>
<div id="app">
<div class="toolbar">
  <div class="toolbar-row">
    <span class="title">z = ax³ + bx - y²</span>
    <div class="radio-group">
      <label class="radio-label" data-value="a"><input type="radio" name="param" value="a"> a</label>
      <label class="radio-label" data-value="b"><input type="radio" name="param" value="b"> b</label>
      <label class="radio-label active" data-value="z"><input type="radio" name="param" value="z" checked> z</label>
    </div>
    <span id="param-val" class="param-val">0.00</span>
    <div style="flex:1;"></div>
    <button id="btn-ecc" class="btn btn-ecc">ECC</button>
  </div>
  <div class="toolbar-row"><input id="slider" type="range" min="-20" max="20" step="0.001" value="0"></div>
  <div class="toolbar-row">
    <button id="btn-play" class="btn btn-primary">▶ Play</button>
    <button id="btn-dir" class="btn btn-secondary">↓ Down</button>
    <div style="flex:1;"></div>
    <div style="display:flex;align-items:center;gap:8px;margin-left:12px;">
      <span style="font-size:13px;color:#9ca3af;font-weight:600;">θ</span>
      <input id="theta-slider" type="range" min="0" max="180" step="0.1" value="0" style="width:140px;accent-color:#fbbf24;">
      <span id="theta-val" class="param-val" style="min-width:50px;color:#fbbf24;">0.0°</span>
    </div>
  </div>
</div>
<div class="main">
  <div class="panel" id="panel-3d">
    <div class="panel-header" id="header-3d">
      <div class="left">
        <span class="icon" id="icon-3d">▼</span>
        <span class="name">3D Surface & Section</span>
        <span class="hint" id="hint-3d">— z = -1.00x³ + 1.00x - y²</span>
      </div>
    </div>
    <div class="panel-body" id="body-3d"><div id="three-container"></div></div>
  </div>
  <div class="panel collapsed" id="panel-2d">
    <div class="panel-header" id="header-2d">
      <div class="left">
        <span class="icon" id="icon-2d">▼</span>
        <span class="name">2D Section Curve</span>
        <span class="hint" id="formula-hint">— y² = -1.00x³ + 1.00x + 0.00</span>
      </div>
    </div>
    <div class="panel-body" id="body-2d" style="position:relative;">
      <canvas id="canvas-2d"></canvas>
      <div id="ecc-step-hint" class="ecc-step-hint"></div>
      <div id="ecc-controls" class="ecc-controls hidden">
        <div class="ecc-row">
          <button id="ecc-qige" class="btn btn-qige">Qige</button>
          <button id="ecc-spencer" class="btn btn-spencer">Spencer</button>
          <span class="ecc-label">Private Key:</span>
          <input id="ecc-priv" class="ecc-input" type="text" inputmode="numeric" pattern="[0-9]*" enterkeyhint="done" placeholder="1-499">
          <button id="ecc-run" class="btn btn-run">Run</button>
          <button id="ecc-play" class="btn btn-secondary">▶ Auto</button>
          <div style="flex:1;"></div>
        </div>
        <div class="ecc-row">
          <span class="ecc-label">Binary:</span>
          <span id="ecc-bits" class="ecc-bits"></span>
        </div>
        <div class="ecc-row">
          <span class="ecc-label">Progress:</span>
          <div class="ecc-progress-wrap">
            <input id="ecc-progress" class="ecc-progress" type="range" min="0" max="0" step="1" value="0">
            <span id="ecc-step-label" class="ecc-step-label">0 / 0</span>
          </div>
        </div>
        <div class="ecc-row" id="ecc-result-row" style="display:none;">
          <span class="ecc-result" id="ecc-result-text"></span>
        </div>
        <div class="ecc-row" id="ecc-compare-row" style="display:none;">
          <div class="ecc-compare" id="ecc-compare"></div>
        </div>
        <div class="ecc-row">
          <span id="ecc-hint" class="ecc-hint">Press ECC button to enter ECC mode.</span>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  </div>
</div>
</div>

<script type="importmap">
{
  "imports": {
    "three": "https://unpkg.com/three@0.160.0/build/three.module.js",
    "three/addons/": "https://unpkg.com/three@0.160.0/examples/jsm/"
  }
}
</script>
<script type="module">
import * as THREE from "three";
import { OrbitControls } from "three/addons/controls/OrbitControls.js";

let a = -1, b = 1, zVal = 0, theta = 0;
let activeParam = "z";
let isPlaying = false, playDir = 1;
let animId = null, lastTime = 0;

const slider = document.getElementById("slider");
const paramVal = document.getElementById("param-val");
const formulaHint = document.getElementById("formula-hint");
const btnPlay = document.getElementById("btn-play");
const btnDir = document.getElementById("btn-dir");
const thetaSlider = document.getElementById("theta-slider");
const thetaVal = document.getElementById("theta-val");

const canvas2d = document.getElementById("canvas-2d");
const ctx2d = canvas2d.getContext("2d");
const xMin = -15, xMax = 15, yMin2D = -15, yMax2D = 15;

/* ===================== 2D View Transform (pan/zoom) ===================== */
let viewScale = 1;
let viewPanX = 0;
let viewPanY = 0;

function toScreen(x, y) {
  const w = canvas2d.width / (window.devicePixelRatio || 1);
  const h = canvas2d.height / (window.devicePixelRatio || 1);
  const baseScale = Math.min(w / (xMax - xMin), h / (yMax2D - yMin2D));
  const scale = baseScale * viewScale;
  const cx = w / 2 + viewPanX;
  const cy = h / 2 + viewPanY;
  return [cx + x * scale, cy - y * scale];
}

function toMath(sx, sy) {
  const w = canvas2d.width / (window.devicePixelRatio || 1);
  const h = canvas2d.height / (window.devicePixelRatio || 1);
  const baseScale = Math.min(w / (xMax - xMin), h / (yMax2D - yMin2D));
  const scale = baseScale * viewScale;
  const cx = w / 2 + viewPanX;
  const cy = h / 2 + viewPanY;
  return [(sx - cx) / scale, (cy - sy) / scale];
}

/* ===================== Touch gestures for 2D ===================== */
let touchState = { active: false, startX: 0, startY: 0, lastX: 0, lastY: 0, startDist: 0, isPinch: false, moved: false };

canvas2d.addEventListener('touchstart', (e) => {
  if (e.touches.length === 1) {
    touchState.active = true;
    touchState.isPinch = false;
    touchState.moved = false;
    touchState.startX = touchState.lastX = e.touches[0].clientX;
    touchState.startY = touchState.lastY = e.touches[0].clientY;
  } else if (e.touches.length === 2) {
    touchState.isPinch = true;
    touchState.active = false;
    const dx = e.touches[0].clientX - e.touches[1].clientX;
    const dy = e.touches[0].clientY - e.touches[1].clientY;
    touchState.startDist = Math.sqrt(dx*dx + dy*dy);
    touchState.lastX = (e.touches[0].clientX + e.touches[1].clientX) / 2;
    touchState.lastY = (e.touches[0].clientY + e.touches[1].clientY) / 2;
  }
}, {passive: false});

canvas2d.addEventListener('touchmove', (e) => {
  if ((!touchState.active && !touchState.isPinch) || e.touches.length === 0) return;
  e.preventDefault();
  if (e.touches.length === 1 && !touchState.isPinch) {
    const dx = e.touches[0].clientX - touchState.lastX;
    const dy = e.touches[0].clientY - touchState.lastY;
    viewPanX += dx;
    viewPanY += dy;
    touchState.lastX = e.touches[0].clientX;
    touchState.lastY = e.touches[0].clientY;
    if (Math.abs(dx) > 2 || Math.abs(dy) > 2) touchState.moved = true;
    draw2D();
  } else if (e.touches.length === 2 && touchState.isPinch) {
    const dx = e.touches[0].clientX - e.touches[1].clientX;
    const dy = e.touches[0].clientY - e.touches[1].clientY;
    const dist = Math.sqrt(dx*dx + dy*dy);
    if (touchState.startDist > 0) {
      const scaleFactor = dist / touchState.startDist;
      viewScale *= scaleFactor;
      viewScale = Math.max(0.2, Math.min(15, viewScale));
    }
    touchState.startDist = dist;
    const midX = (e.touches[0].clientX + e.touches[1].clientX) / 2;
    const midY = (e.touches[0].clientY + e.touches[1].clientY) / 2;
    viewPanX += (midX - touchState.lastX);
    viewPanY += (midY - touchState.lastY);
    touchState.lastX = midX;
    touchState.lastY = midY;
    draw2D();
  }
}, {passive: false});

canvas2d.addEventListener('touchend', (e) => {
  if (e.touches.length === 0) {
    if (touchState.active && !touchState.moved && !touchState.isPinch) {
      const rect = canvas2d.getBoundingClientRect();
      const sx = touchState.startX - rect.left;
      const sy = touchState.startY - rect.top;
      handleCanvasClick(sx, sy);
    }
    touchState.active = false;
    touchState.isPinch = false;
  } else if (e.touches.length === 1 && touchState.isPinch) {
    touchState.isPinch = false;
    touchState.active = true;
    touchState.lastX = e.touches[0].clientX;
    touchState.lastY = e.touches[0].clientY;
    touchState.moved = true;
  }
});

/* ===================== ECC State ===================== */
const ECC = {
  active: false,
  phase: 'idle',
  base: null,
  baseStd: null,
  qige: { sk: null, pk: null, ss: null },
  spencer: { sk: null, pk: null, ss: null },
  role: null,
  target: null,
  steps: [],
  totalSteps: 0,
  currentStep: 0,
  isAutoPlaying: false,
  autoTimer: null,
  bits: '',
};

const C = Math.pow(5, 1/3);
const A_std = -4.5 * C;
const B_std = 11;

const stepHints = {
  'select_base': 'Step 1: Click on the blue curve to select Base point G',
  'base_selected': 'Step 2: Press Qige or Spencer, then enter private key',
  'qige_wait': 'Step 2: Qige — Enter private key (1-499) and press Run',
  'qige_run': 'Step 2: Qige — Computing public key A...',
  'qige_done': 'Step 3: Spencer — Press Spencer button to generate public key B',
  'spencer_wait': 'Step 3: Spencer — Enter private key (1-499) and press Run',
  'spencer_run': 'Step 3: Spencer — Computing public key B...',
  'spencer_done': 'Step 4: Qige — Press Qige, enter new private key for shared secret',
  'shared_qige_wait': 'Step 4: Qige — Enter private key (1-499) to compute shared secret',
  'shared_qige_run': 'Step 4: Qige — Computing shared secret...',
  'shared_qige_done': 'Step 5: Spencer — Press Spencer, enter new private key for shared secret',
  'shared_spencer_wait': 'Step 5: Spencer — Enter private key (1-499) to compute shared secret',
  'shared_spencer_run': 'Step 5: Spencer — Computing shared secret...',
  'shared_spencer_done': 'Done! Both shared secrets match. Press Spencer to reset.',
};

const eccControls = document.getElementById("ecc-controls");
const eccStepHint = document.getElementById("ecc-step-hint");
const eccQige = document.getElementById("ecc-qige");
const eccSpencer = document.getElementById("ecc-spencer");
const eccPriv = document.getElementById("ecc-priv");
const eccRun = document.getElementById("ecc-run");
const eccPlay = document.getElementById("ecc-play");
const eccBits = document.getElementById("ecc-bits");
const eccProgress = document.getElementById("ecc-progress");
const eccStepLabel = document.getElementById("ecc-step-label");
const eccResultRow = document.getElementById("ecc-result-row");
const eccResultText = document.getElementById("ecc-result-text");
const eccCompareRow = document.getElementById("ecc-compare-row");
const eccCompare = document.getElementById("ecc-compare");
const eccHint = document.getElementById("ecc-hint");

/* ===================== Click-to-increment ===================== */
function setupClickIncrement(el, getVal, setVal, step, getMin, getMax) {
  let intervalId = null, timeoutId = null, started = false;
  const doStep = () => {
    let v = getVal() + step;
    const min = getMin ? getMin() : undefined;
    const max = getMax ? getMax() : undefined;
    if (min !== undefined) v = Math.max(min, v);
    if (max !== undefined) v = Math.min(max, v);
    setVal(v);
  };
  const startRepeat = () => { if (started) return; started = true; doStep(); timeoutId = setTimeout(() => { intervalId = setInterval(doStep, 80); }, 400); };
  const stopRepeat = () => { started = false; clearTimeout(timeoutId); clearInterval(intervalId); timeoutId = null; intervalId = null; };
  el.addEventListener("mousedown", (e) => { if (e.button !== 0) return; e.preventDefault(); startRepeat(); });
  el.addEventListener("touchstart", (e) => { e.preventDefault(); startRepeat(); }, { passive: false });
  el.addEventListener("mouseup", stopRepeat);
  el.addEventListener("mouseleave", stopRepeat);
  el.addEventListener("touchend", stopRepeat);
  el.addEventListener("touchcancel", stopRepeat);
}

/* ===================== 2D helpers ===================== */
function resize2D() {
  const rect = canvas2d.parentElement.getBoundingClientRect();
  const dpr = window.devicePixelRatio || 1;
  canvas2d.width = rect.width * dpr;
  canvas2d.height = rect.height * dpr;
  ctx2d.setTransform(dpr, 0, 0, dpr, 0, 0);
  draw2D();
}

function drawGrid2D() {
  const w = canvas2d.width / (window.devicePixelRatio || 1);
  const h = canvas2d.height / (window.devicePixelRatio || 1);
  ctx2d.clearRect(0, 0, w, h);
  ctx2d.fillStyle = "#0b0f19";
  ctx2d.fillRect(0, 0, w, h);
  ctx2d.strokeStyle = "#1f2937"; ctx2d.lineWidth = 1;
  for (let x = Math.ceil(xMin); x <= Math.floor(xMax); x++) {
    const [sx1, sy1] = toScreen(x, yMin2D);
    const [sx2, sy2] = toScreen(x, yMax2D);
    ctx2d.beginPath(); ctx2d.moveTo(sx1, sy1); ctx2d.lineTo(sx2, sy2); ctx2d.stroke();
  }
  for (let y = Math.ceil(yMin2D); y <= Math.floor(yMax2D); y++) {
    const [sx1, sy1] = toScreen(xMin, y);
    const [sx2, sy2] = toScreen(xMax, y);
    ctx2d.beginPath(); ctx2d.moveTo(sx1, sy1); ctx2d.lineTo(sx2, sy2); ctx2d.stroke();
  }
  ctx2d.strokeStyle = "#4b5563"; ctx2d.lineWidth = 1.5;
  const [ox1, oy1] = toScreen(xMin, 0), [ox2, oy2] = toScreen(xMax, 0);
  ctx2d.beginPath(); ctx2d.moveTo(ox1, oy1); ctx2d.lineTo(ox2, oy2); ctx2d.stroke();
  const [oyx1, oyy1] = toScreen(0, yMin2D), [oyx2, oyy2] = toScreen(0, yMax2D);
  ctx2d.beginPath(); ctx2d.moveTo(oyx1, oyy1); ctx2d.lineTo(oyx2, oyy2); ctx2d.stroke();
}

function getIntervals(aa, bb, zz, thDeg) {
  const th = thDeg * Math.PI / 180;
  const cosT = Math.cos(th);
  const steps = 4000;
  const dxx = (xMax - xMin) / steps;
  function f(x) {
    if (Math.abs(cosT) < 1e-6) return -1;
    return aa * x * x * x + bb * x - x * Math.tan(th) - zz / cosT;
  }
  if (Math.abs(cosT) < 1e-6) {
    const xFixed = -zz / Math.sin(th);
    if (xFixed >= xMin && xFixed <= xMax) return [{ left: xFixed, right: xFixed, vertical: true }];
    return [];
  }
  const roots = [];
  for (let i = 0; i <= steps; i++) {
    const x = xMin + i * dxx, fx = f(x);
    if (Math.abs(fx) < 1e-10) roots.push(x);
  }
  let prev = f(xMin);
  for (let i = 1; i <= steps; i++) {
    const x = xMin + i * dxx, curr = f(x);
    if (prev * curr < 0) {
      let lo = x - dxx, hi = x;
      for (let k = 0; k < 25; k++) {
        const mid = (lo + hi) / 2, vm = f(mid);
        if (Math.abs(vm) < 1e-12) { lo = hi = mid; break; }
        if (prev * vm < 0) hi = mid; else lo = mid;
      }
      roots.push((lo + hi) / 2);
    }
    prev = curr;
  }
  const uniq = [];
  for (const r of roots) { if (!uniq.some(u => Math.abs(u - r) < 0.001)) uniq.push(r); }
  uniq.sort((p, q) => p - q);
  const pts = [xMin, ...uniq, xMax];
  const intervals = [];
  for (let i = 0; i < pts.length - 1; i++) {
    const mid = (pts[i] + pts[i + 1]) / 2;
    if (f(mid) > 0) intervals.push({ left: pts[i], right: pts[i + 1] });
  }
  return intervals;
}

function drawSection2D() {
  const th = theta * Math.PI / 180;
  const cosT = Math.cos(th);
  const intervals = getIntervals(a, b, zVal, theta);
  ctx2d.strokeStyle = "#60a5fa"; ctx2d.lineWidth = 2.5;
  if (intervals.length === 1 && intervals[0].vertical) {
    const xFixed = intervals[0].left;
    const [sx, syTop] = toScreen(xFixed, yMax2D);
    const [sx2, syBot] = toScreen(xFixed, yMin2D);
    ctx2d.beginPath(); ctx2d.moveTo(sx, syTop); ctx2d.lineTo(sx2, syBot); ctx2d.stroke();
    if (!ECC.active) {
      ctx2d.fillStyle = "#fbbf24"; ctx2d.font = "12px sans-serif"; ctx2d.textAlign = "center";
      ctx2d.fillText(`x = ${xFixed.toFixed(2)}`, sx, syTop - 8);
    }
    return;
  }
  intervals.forEach(({ left, right }) => {
    const segSteps = Math.max(2, Math.ceil((right - left) / (xMax - xMin) * 800));
    const ddx = (right - left) / segSteps;
    const upper = [], lower = [];
    for (let i = 0; i <= segSteps; i++) {
      const x = left + i * ddx;
      const fx = a * x * x * x + b * x - x * Math.tan(th) - zVal / cosT;
      const y = Math.sqrt(Math.max(0, fx));
      upper.push(toScreen(x, y));
      lower.push(toScreen(x, -y));
    }
    const leftIsRoot = Math.abs(left - xMin) > 0.001;
    const rightIsRoot = Math.abs(right - xMax) > 0.001;
    const isClosed = leftIsRoot && rightIsRoot;
    if (isClosed) {
      const pts = [...upper, ...lower.reverse()];
      if (pts.length > 2) { ctx2d.beginPath(); ctx2d.moveTo(pts[0][0], pts[0][1]); for (let i = 1; i < pts.length; i++) ctx2d.lineTo(pts[i][0], pts[i][1]); ctx2d.stroke(); }
    } else {
      if (upper.length > 1) { ctx2d.beginPath(); ctx2d.moveTo(upper[0][0], upper[0][1]); for (let i = 1; i < upper.length; i++) ctx2d.lineTo(upper[i][0], upper[i][1]); ctx2d.stroke(); }
      if (lower.length > 1) { ctx2d.beginPath(); ctx2d.moveTo(lower[0][0], lower[0][1]); for (let i = 1; i < lower.length; i++) ctx2d.lineTo(lower[i][0], lower[i][1]); ctx2d.stroke(); }
    }
  });
  if (!ECC.active) {
    ctx2d.fillStyle = "#fbbf24";
    const roots = [];
    intervals.forEach(({ left, right }) => {
      if (Math.abs(left - xMin) > 0.001) roots.push(left);
      if (Math.abs(right - xMax) > 0.001) roots.push(right);
    });
    const uniqRoots = [];
    for (const r of roots) { if (!uniqRoots.some(u => Math.abs(u - r) < 0.001)) uniqRoots.push(r); }
    for (const r of uniqRoots) {
      if (r >= xMin && r <= xMax) {
        const [sx, sy] = toScreen(r, 0);
        ctx2d.beginPath(); ctx2d.arc(sx, sy, 2, 0, Math.PI * 2); ctx2d.fill();
      }
    }
  }
}

function draw2D() {
  drawGrid2D();
  drawSection2D();
  if (ECC.active) drawECCOverlay();
}

/* ===================== ECC Math ===================== */
function toStd(x, y) { return { X: x / C, Y: y }; }
function fromStd(X, Y) { return { x: X * C, y: Y }; }

function ecDouble(P) {
  if (P.isInfinity) return { isInfinity: true };
  const lam = (3 * P.X * P.X + A_std) / (2 * P.Y);
  const X3 = lam * lam - 2 * P.X;
  const Y3 = lam * (P.X - X3) - P.Y;
  return { X: X3, Y: Y3 };
}

function ecAdd(P, Q) {
  if (P.isInfinity) return { ...Q };
  if (Q.isInfinity) return { ...P };
  if (Math.abs(P.X - Q.X) < 1e-10 && Math.abs(P.Y + Q.Y) < 1e-10) return { isInfinity: true };
  const lam = (Q.Y - P.Y) / (Q.X - P.X);
  const X3 = lam * lam - P.X - Q.X;
  const Y3 = lam * (P.X - X3) - P.Y;
  return { X: X3, Y: Y3 };
}

function getTangentLine(P) {
  if (P.isInfinity) return null;
  const lam = (3 * P.X * P.X + A_std) / (2 * P.Y);
  const c = P.Y - lam * P.X;
  return { lam, c };
}

function getChordLine(P, Q) {
  if (P.isInfinity || Q.isInfinity) return null;
  const lam = (Q.Y - P.Y) / (Q.X - P.X);
  const c = P.Y - lam * P.X;
  return { lam, c };
}

function getThirdIntersection(lam, c, X1, X2) {
  const X3 = lam * lam - X1 - X2;
  const Y3 = lam * X3 + c;
  return { X: X3, Y: Y3 };
}

function precomputeSteps(startStd, n, baseStd) {
  const bits = n.toString(2).split('');
  const steps = [];
  let R = { ...startStd };
  for (let i = 0; i < bits.length; i++) {
    const lineD = getTangentLine(R);
    const R2 = ecDouble(R);
    let intD = null;
    if (lineD) {
      const X1 = R.isInfinity ? 0 : R.X;
      intD = getThirdIntersection(lineD.lam, lineD.c, X1, X1);
    }
    steps.push({
      type: 'double', bitIndex: i, bit: bits[i],
      from: { ...R }, to: { ...R2 },
      line: lineD, intersection: intD,
    });
    R = { ...R2 };
    if (bits[i] === '1') {
      const lineA = getChordLine(R, baseStd);
      const Radd = ecAdd(R, baseStd);
      let intA = null;
      if (lineA) {
        const fromX = R.isInfinity ? 0 : R.X;
        const baseX = baseStd.isInfinity ? 0 : baseStd.X;
        intA = getThirdIntersection(lineA.lam, lineA.c, fromX, baseX);
      }
      steps.push({
        type: 'add', bitIndex: i, bit: bits[i],
        from: { ...R }, to: { ...Radd }, base: { ...baseStd },
        line: lineA, intersection: intA,
      });
      R = { ...Radd };
    }
  }
  return { bits: bits.join(''), steps, result: R };
}

/* ===================== ECC Drawing ===================== */
function drawPointDisp(pt, color, label, radius) {
  const r = radius || 4;
  const [sx, sy] = toScreen(pt.x, pt.y);
  ctx2d.fillStyle = color;
  ctx2d.beginPath();
  ctx2d.arc(sx, sy, r, 0, Math.PI * 2);
  ctx2d.fill();
  if (label) {
    ctx2d.fillStyle = color;
    ctx2d.font = "bold 12px sans-serif";
    ctx2d.textAlign = "left";
    ctx2d.fillText(label, sx + 8, sy - 8);
  }
}

function drawLineStd(line, color, width, dash) {
  if (!line) return;
  const m = line.lam / C;
  const c = line.c;
  const pts = [];
  if (Math.abs(m) < 1e-8) {
    pts.push(toScreen(xMin, c));
    pts.push(toScreen(xMax, c));
  } else {
    const yAtXmin = m * xMin + c;
    const yAtXmax = m * xMax + c;
    const xAtYmin = (yMin2D - c) / m;
    const xAtYmax = (yMax2D - c) / m;
    const candidates = [[xMin, yAtXmin], [xMax, yAtXmax], [xAtYmin, yMin2D], [xAtYmax, yMax2D]];
    for (const [cx, cy] of candidates) {
      if (cx >= xMin - 1e-6 && cx <= xMax + 1e-6 && cy >= yMin2D - 1e-6 && cy <= yMax2D + 1e-6) {
        pts.push(toScreen(cx, cy));
      }
    }
  }
  if (pts.length >= 2) {
    ctx2d.strokeStyle = color;
    ctx2d.lineWidth = width || 0.6;
    ctx2d.setLineDash(dash || [4, 3]);
    ctx2d.beginPath();
    ctx2d.moveTo(pts[0][0], pts[0][1]);
    ctx2d.lineTo(pts[1][0], pts[1][1]);
    ctx2d.stroke();
    ctx2d.setLineDash([]);
  }
}

function drawReflectionLine(x, color, width) {
  const [ix, iyTop] = toScreen(x, yMax2D);
  const [ix2, iyBot] = toScreen(x, yMin2D);
  ctx2d.strokeStyle = color || "#6b7280";
  ctx2d.lineWidth = width || 0.5;
  ctx2d.setLineDash([2, 2]);
  ctx2d.beginPath();
  ctx2d.moveTo(ix, iyTop);
  ctx2d.lineTo(ix2, iyBot);
  ctx2d.stroke();
  ctx2d.setLineDash([]);
}

function drawECCOverlay() {
  if (!ECC.base) return;

  drawPointDisp(ECC.base, "#ef4444", "G", 4);
  if (ECC.qige.pk) drawPointDisp(fromStd(ECC.qige.pk.X, ECC.qige.pk.Y), "#3b82f6", "A", 4);
  if (ECC.spencer.pk) drawPointDisp(fromStd(ECC.spencer.pk.X, ECC.spencer.pk.Y), "#22c55e", "B", 4);
  if (ECC.qige.ss) drawPointDisp(fromStd(ECC.qige.ss.X, ECC.qige.ss.Y), "#eab308", "S", 4);

  const histSteps = Math.min(ECC.currentStep, ECC.steps.length);
  for (let i = 0; i < histSteps; i++) {
    const step = ECC.steps[i];
    if (!step.from.isInfinity && step.line) {
      drawLineStd(step.line, "#9ca3af", 0.5, [3, 4]);
    }
    if (step.type === 'double' && !step.from.isInfinity) {
      const tp = fromStd(step.from.X, step.from.Y);
      drawPointDisp(tp, "#ff00ff", "", 2);
    }
    if (step.intersection && !step.intersection.isInfinity) {
      const ip = fromStd(step.intersection.X, step.intersection.Y);
      drawPointDisp(ip, "#808000", "", 2);
      drawReflectionLine(ip.x, "#9ca3af", 0.5);
    }
    if (!step.to.isInfinity) {
      const rp = fromStd(step.to.X, step.to.Y);
      drawPointDisp(rp, "#ee82ee", "", 2);
    }
  }

  if (ECC.currentStep > 0 && ECC.currentStep <= ECC.steps.length) {
    const cur = ECC.steps[ECC.currentStep - 1];
    if (cur && !cur.from.isInfinity && cur.line) {
      const lineColor = cur.type === 'double' ? "#f87171" : "#60a5fa";
      drawLineStd(cur.line, lineColor, 1.2, [4, 3]);
    }
    // Prominent current point
    if (cur && !cur.to.isInfinity) {
      const tp = fromStd(cur.to.X, cur.to.Y);
      const [sx, sy] = toScreen(tp.x, tp.y);
      ctx2d.strokeStyle = "#ffffff";
      ctx2d.lineWidth = 2;
      ctx2d.beginPath();
      ctx2d.arc(sx, sy, 7, 0, Math.PI * 2);
      ctx2d.stroke();
      drawPointDisp(tp, cur.type === 'double' ? "#f87171" : "#60a5fa", "", 5);
    }
    // Identity operation hint
    if (cur && cur.from.isInfinity) {
      ctx2d.fillStyle = "rgba(255,255,255,0.8)";
      ctx2d.font = "bold 12px sans-serif";
      ctx2d.textAlign = "left";
      const label = cur.type === 'double' ? "∞ · 2 = ∞" : "∞ + G = G";
      ctx2d.fillText(label, 12, 22);
    }
  }
}

/* ===================== ECC UI Logic ===================== */
function updateStepHint() {
  const text = stepHints[ECC.phase] || '';
  eccStepHint.textContent = text;
  if (ECC.active && text) {
    eccStepHint.classList.add('visible');
  } else {
    eccStepHint.classList.remove('visible');
  }
}

function setECCHint(msg) {
  eccHint.textContent = msg;
}

function updateECCUI() {
  const running = ECC.phase.endsWith('_run');
  const isWait = ECC.phase.endsWith('_wait');

  eccQige.disabled = running || ECC.phase === 'select_base' || (ECC.phase === 'idle' && !ECC.base);
  eccSpencer.disabled = running || ECC.phase === 'select_base';

  eccQige.classList.toggle('active', ECC.role === 'qige');
  eccSpencer.classList.toggle('active', ECC.role === 'spencer');

  if (isWait) {
    eccPriv.readOnly = false;
    eccPriv.disabled = false;
    eccRun.disabled = true;
    eccPlay.disabled = true;
    const n = parseInt(eccPriv.value, 10);
    if (n && n >= 1 && n < 500) eccRun.disabled = false;
  } else if (running) {
    eccPriv.readOnly = true;
    eccPriv.disabled = true;
    eccRun.disabled = true;
    eccPlay.disabled = false;
  } else {
    eccPriv.readOnly = true;
    eccPriv.disabled = true;
    eccRun.disabled = true;
    eccPlay.disabled = true;
  }

  eccProgress.disabled = ECC.steps.length === 0;

  updateStepHint();

  if (ECC.bits) {
    const arr = ECC.bits.split('');
    let bitIdx = -1;
    if (ECC.currentStep > 0 && ECC.currentStep <= ECC.steps.length) {
      bitIdx = ECC.steps[ECC.currentStep - 1].bitIndex;
    }
    eccBits.innerHTML = arr.map((b, i) => {
      const cls = (i === bitIdx) ? 'active-bit' : '';
      return `<span class="${cls}">${b}</span>`;
    }).join('');
  } else {
    eccBits.textContent = '';
  }

  eccProgress.max = ECC.totalSteps;
  eccProgress.value = ECC.currentStep;
  eccStepLabel.textContent = `${ECC.currentStep} / ${ECC.totalSteps}`;

  if (ECC.phase === 'qige_done' && ECC.qige.pk) {
    const p = fromStd(ECC.qige.pk.X, ECC.qige.pk.Y);
    eccResultText.innerHTML = `Qige Public Key A: <span>(${p.x.toFixed(1)}, ${p.y.toFixed(1)})</span>`;
    eccResultRow.style.display = '';
  } else if (ECC.phase === 'spencer_done' && ECC.spencer.pk) {
    const p = fromStd(ECC.spencer.pk.X, ECC.spencer.pk.Y);
    eccResultText.innerHTML = `Spencer Public Key B: <span>(${p.x.toFixed(1)}, ${p.y.toFixed(1)})</span>`;
    eccResultRow.style.display = '';
  } else if (ECC.phase === 'shared_qige_done' && ECC.qige.ss) {
    const p = fromStd(ECC.qige.ss.X, ECC.qige.ss.Y);
    eccResultText.innerHTML = `Qige Shared Secret S: <span>(${p.x.toFixed(1)}, ${p.y.toFixed(1)})</span>`;
    eccResultRow.style.display = '';
  } else if (ECC.phase === 'shared_spencer_done' && ECC.spencer.ss) {
    const p = fromStd(ECC.spencer.ss.X, ECC.spencer.ss.Y);
    eccResultText.innerHTML = `Spencer Shared Secret S: <span>(${p.x.toFixed(1)}, ${p.y.toFixed(1)})</span>`;
    eccResultRow.style.display = '';
  } else {
    eccResultRow.style.display = 'none';
  }

  if (ECC.phase === 'shared_spencer_done') {
    const qa = ECC.qige.pk ? fromStd(ECC.qige.pk.X, ECC.qige.pk.Y) : null;
    const qb = ECC.spencer.pk ? fromStd(ECC.spencer.pk.X, ECC.spencer.pk.Y) : null;
    const qs = ECC.qige.ss ? fromStd(ECC.qige.ss.X, ECC.qige.ss.Y) : null;
    const ss = ECC.spencer.ss ? fromStd(ECC.spencer.ss.X, ECC.spencer.ss.Y) : null;
    const match = qs && ss && Math.abs(qs.x - ss.x) < 0.05 && Math.abs(qs.y - ss.y) < 0.05;
    eccCompare.innerHTML = `
      <div class="ecc-compare-col">
        <div class="ecc-compare-title">Qige</div>
        <div>sk: ${ECC.qige.sk || '-'}</div>
        <div>pk: ${qa ? `(${qa.x.toFixed(1)}, ${qa.y.toFixed(1)})` : '-'}</div>
        <div>ss: ${qs ? `(${qs.x.toFixed(1)}, ${qs.y.toFixed(1)})` : '-'}</div>
      </div>
      <div class="ecc-compare-col">
        <div class="ecc-compare-title">Spencer</div>
        <div>sk: ${ECC.spencer.sk || '-'}</div>
        <div>pk: ${qb ? `(${qb.x.toFixed(1)}, ${qb.y.toFixed(1)})` : '-'}</div>
        <div>ss: ${ss ? `(${ss.x.toFixed(1)}, ${ss.y.toFixed(1)})` : '-'}</div>
      </div>
      <div class="ecc-compare-col" style="color:${match ? '#22c55e' : '#ef4444'};font-weight:700;">
        <div>Shared Secret Match!</div>
        <div>${match ? '✓ Identical' : '✗ Mismatch'}</div>
      </div>
    `;
    eccCompareRow.style.display = '';
  } else {
    eccCompareRow.style.display = 'none';
  }
}

function startECCRole(role, target) {
  ECC.role = role;
  ECC.target = target;
  ECC.steps = [];
  ECC.currentStep = 0;
  ECC.totalSteps = 0;
  ECC.bits = '';
  ECC.isAutoPlaying = false;
  if (ECC.autoTimer) { clearInterval(ECC.autoTimer); ECC.autoTimer = null; }
  eccPlay.textContent = "▶ Auto";
  eccPriv.value = '';
  setECCHint(`${role === 'qige' ? 'Qige' : 'Spencer'}: Enter private key (1-499) and press Run.`);
}

function runECC() {
  const n = parseInt(eccPriv.value, 10);
  if (!n || n < 1 || n >= 500) {
    setECCHint("Please enter a private key between 1 and 499.");
    return;
  }
  let startPt, basePt;
  if (ECC.target === 'pub') {
    startPt = { isInfinity: true };
    basePt = { ...ECC.baseStd };
    if (ECC.role === 'qige') ECC.qige.sk = n;
    else ECC.spencer.sk = n;
  } else {
    if (ECC.role === 'qige') {
      if (!ECC.spencer.pk) { setECCHint("Spencer's public key not available yet."); return; }
      startPt = { isInfinity: true };
      basePt = { ...ECC.spencer.pk };
      ECC.qige.sk = n;
    } else {
      if (!ECC.qige.pk) { setECCHint("Qige's public key not available yet."); return; }
      startPt = { isInfinity: true };
      basePt = { ...ECC.qige.pk };
      ECC.spencer.sk = n;
    }
  }
  const pre = precomputeSteps(startPt, n, basePt);
  ECC.steps = pre.steps;
  ECC.bits = pre.bits;
  ECC.totalSteps = pre.steps.length;
  ECC.currentStep = 0;
  ECC.phase = ECC.phase.replace('_wait', '_run');
  eccProgress.max = ECC.totalSteps;
  setECCHint("Use progress bar to step through or press Auto to play.");
  updateECCUI();
  draw2D();
  setTimeout(() => { if (ECC.phase.endsWith('_run')) startAutoPlay(); }, 500);
}

function finishECCRun() {
  const last = ECC.steps.length > 0 ? ECC.steps[ECC.steps.length - 1].to : { isInfinity: true };
  if (ECC.role === 'qige') {
    if (ECC.target === 'pub') {
      ECC.qige.pk = { ...last };
      ECC.phase = 'qige_done';
      setECCHint("Qige public key A generated. Press Spencer to continue.");
    } else {
      ECC.qige.ss = { ...last };
      ECC.phase = 'shared_qige_done';
      setECCHint("Qige shared secret computed. Press Spencer to compute shared secret.");
    }
  } else {
    if (ECC.target === 'pub') {
      ECC.spencer.pk = { ...last };
      ECC.phase = 'spencer_done';
      setECCHint("Spencer public key B generated. Press Qige to compute shared secret.");
    } else {
      ECC.spencer.ss = { ...last };
      ECC.phase = 'shared_spencer_done';
      setECCHint("Complete! Both shared secrets match. Press Spencer to reset.");
    }
  }
  updateECCUI();
  draw2D();
}

function setStep(s) {
  s = Math.max(0, Math.min(ECC.totalSteps, s));
  ECC.currentStep = s;
  if (s >= ECC.totalSteps && ECC.phase.endsWith('_run')) {
    finishECCRun();
  } else {
    if (ECC.phase.endsWith('_run') && s > 0 && s <= ECC.steps.length) {
      const step = ECC.steps[s - 1];
      const typeLabel = step.type === 'double' ? 'Double' : 'Add';
      setECCHint(`Step ${s}/${ECC.totalSteps}: ${typeLabel} (bit ${step.bitIndex})`);
    }
    updateECCUI();
    draw2D();
  }
}

function startAutoPlay() {
  if (ECC.isAutoPlaying) return;
  ECC.isAutoPlaying = true;
  eccPlay.textContent = "⏸ Pause";
  ECC.autoTimer = setInterval(() => {
    if (!ECC.isAutoPlaying) return;
    if (ECC.currentStep >= ECC.totalSteps) { stopAutoPlay(); return; }
    setStep(ECC.currentStep + 1);
  }, 900);
}

function stopAutoPlay() {
  ECC.isAutoPlaying = false;
  eccPlay.textContent = "▶ Auto";
  if (ECC.autoTimer) { clearInterval(ECC.autoTimer); ECC.autoTimer = null; }
}

function resetECC() {
  ECC.phase = 'base_selected';
  ECC.role = null;
  ECC.qige = { sk: null, pk: null, ss: null };
  ECC.spencer = { sk: null, pk: null, ss: null };
  ECC.steps = [];
  ECC.currentStep = 0;
  ECC.totalSteps = 0;
  ECC.bits = '';
  stopAutoPlay();
  eccPriv.value = '';
  eccPriv.readOnly = false;
  eccPriv.disabled = false;
  setECCHint("Press Qige or Spencer to begin. Click on the curve to select Base point.");
  updateECCUI();
  draw2D();
}

/* ===================== Canvas click handler (ECC base select) ===================== */
function handleCanvasClick(sx, sy) {
  if (!ECC.active || ECC.phase !== 'select_base') return;
  const [mx, my] = toMath(sx, sy);
  const fy = 0.2 * mx * mx * mx - 4.5 * mx + 11;
  if (fy < -1) { setECCHint("No real point at this x. Click on the blue curve."); return; }
  const yCurve = Math.sqrt(Math.max(0, fy));
  const yChosen = Math.abs(my - yCurve) < Math.abs(my + yCurve) ? yCurve : -yCurve;
  const xr = Math.round(mx * 10) / 10;
  const yr = Math.round(yChosen * 10) / 10;
  const fy2 = 0.2 * xr * xr * xr - 4.5 * xr + 11;
  if (fy2 < 0) { setECCHint("Invalid point. Click closer to the curve."); return; }
  const yFinal = Math.sqrt(fy2);
  const yExact = (yr >= 0) ? yFinal : -yFinal;
  ECC.base = { x: xr, y: Math.round(yExact * 10) / 10 };
  ECC.baseStd = toStd(ECC.base.x, ECC.base.y);
  ECC.phase = 'base_selected';
  setECCHint(`Base G = (${ECC.base.x.toFixed(1)}, ${ECC.base.y.toFixed(1)}). Press Qige or Spencer to begin.`);
  updateECCUI();
  draw2D();
}

/* ===================== ECC Event Handlers ===================== */
document.getElementById("btn-ecc").addEventListener("click", () => {
  if (ECC.active) {
    ECC.active = false;
    document.getElementById("btn-ecc").classList.remove("active");
    document.getElementById("btn-ecc").textContent = "ECC";
    btnPlay.disabled = false;
    eccControls.classList.add("hidden");
    eccStepHint.classList.remove('visible');
    document.getElementById("panel-3d").classList.remove("collapsed");
    document.getElementById("panel-2d").classList.add("collapsed");
    slider.disabled = false;
    thetaSlider.disabled = false;
    document.querySelectorAll('input[name="param"]').forEach(r => r.disabled = false);
    document.querySelectorAll(".radio-label").forEach(l => l.style.pointerEvents = "");
    resetECC();
  } else {
    ECC.active = true;
    a = 0.2; b = -4.5; zVal = -11.0; theta = 0;
    viewScale = 1; viewPanX = 0; viewPanY = 0;
    document.getElementById("btn-ecc").classList.add("active");
    document.getElementById("btn-ecc").textContent = "Exit ECC";
    btnPlay.disabled = true;
    eccControls.classList.remove("hidden");
    document.getElementById("panel-3d").classList.add("collapsed");
    document.getElementById("panel-2d").classList.remove("collapsed");
    slider.disabled = true;
    thetaSlider.disabled = true;
    document.querySelectorAll('input[name="param"]').forEach(r => r.disabled = true);
    document.querySelectorAll(".radio-label").forEach(l => l.style.pointerEvents = "none");
    updateSliderConfig();
    updateSurface(); updateSection3D();
    setTimeout(() => { resize2D(); resize3D(); }, 360);
    ECC.phase = 'select_base';
    setECCHint("Click anywhere on the blue curve to select the Base point G.");
    updateECCUI();
    draw2D();
  }
});

eccQige.addEventListener("click", () => {
  if (ECC.phase === 'shared_spencer_done') { resetECC(); return; }
  if (!ECC.base) { setECCHint("Please select a Base point first."); return; }
  if (ECC.phase === 'base_selected' || ECC.phase === 'select_base') {
    ECC.phase = 'qige_wait';
    startECCRole('qige', 'pub');
  } else if (ECC.phase === 'spencer_done') {
    ECC.phase = 'shared_qige_wait';
    startECCRole('qige', 'shared');
  } else if (ECC.phase === 'shared_qige_done') {
    setECCHint("Qige shared secret already computed. Proceed to Spencer's shared secret.");
    updateECCUI();
    return;
  }
  updateECCUI();
  setTimeout(() => { if (!eccPriv.disabled) eccPriv.focus(); }, 150);
});

eccSpencer.addEventListener("click", () => {
  if (ECC.phase === 'shared_spencer_done') { resetECC(); return; }
  if (!ECC.base) { setECCHint("Please select a Base point first."); return; }
  if (ECC.phase === 'base_selected' || ECC.phase === 'select_base' || ECC.phase === 'qige_done') {
    ECC.phase = 'spencer_wait';
    startECCRole('spencer', 'pub');
  } else if (ECC.phase === 'shared_qige_done') {
    ECC.phase = 'shared_spencer_wait';
    startECCRole('spencer', 'shared');
  }
  updateECCUI();
  setTimeout(() => { if (!eccPriv.disabled) eccPriv.focus(); }, 150);
});

eccPriv.addEventListener("input", () => {
  if (ECC.phase.endsWith('_wait')) {
    const n = parseInt(eccPriv.value, 10);
    eccRun.disabled = (!n || n < 1 || n >= 500);
  }
});

eccPriv.addEventListener("keydown", (e) => {
  if (e.key === "Enter" && !eccRun.disabled && ECC.phase.endsWith('_wait')) {
    runECC();
  }
});

eccRun.addEventListener("click", () => {
  if (ECC.phase.endsWith('_wait')) {
    runECC();
  }
});

eccPlay.addEventListener("click", () => {
  if (ECC.isAutoPlaying) {
    stopAutoPlay();
  } else {
    if (ECC.currentStep >= ECC.totalSteps) {
      setStep(0);
      setTimeout(startAutoPlay, 100);
    } else {
      startAutoPlay();
    }
  }
});

eccProgress.addEventListener("input", () => {
  stopAutoPlay();
  setStep(parseInt(eccProgress.value, 10));
});

canvas2d.addEventListener("click", (e) => {
  const rect = canvas2d.getBoundingClientRect();
  handleCanvasClick(e.clientX - rect.left, e.clientY - rect.top);
});

/* ===================== 3D Scene ===================== */
const container3d = document.getElementById("three-container");
const scene = new THREE.Scene();
scene.background = new THREE.Color(0x0b0f19);
const camera = new THREE.PerspectiveCamera(45, 1, 0.1, 100);
camera.position.set(7, 5, 7);
const renderer = new THREE.WebGLRenderer({ antialias: true });
renderer.setPixelRatio(window.devicePixelRatio || 1);
container3d.appendChild(renderer.domElement);
const controls = new OrbitControls(camera, renderer.domElement);
controls.enableDamping = true; controls.dampingFactor = 0.08;

scene.add(new THREE.AmbientLight(0xffffff, 0.4));
const dirLight = new THREE.DirectionalLight(0xffffff, 0.8);
dirLight.position.set(5, 8, 5); scene.add(dirLight);
const dirLight2 = new THREE.DirectionalLight(0x60a5fa, 0.3);
dirLight2.position.set(-5, 2, -5); scene.add(dirLight2);

const axesHelper = new THREE.AxesHelper(1.33);
scene.add(axesHelper);

const xyPlaneGeo = new THREE.PlaneGeometry(20, 20);
const xyPlaneMat = new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ color: 0x1f2937, side: THREE.DoubleSide, transparent: true, opacity: 0.25 });
const xyPlane = new THREE.Mesh(xyPlaneGeo, xyPlaneMat);
xyPlane.rotation.x = -Math.PI / 2; xyPlane.position.y = 0;
scene.add(xyPlane);

const gridSize = 20, gridDivs = 20;
const gridGeo = new THREE.BufferGeometry();
const gridPts = [];
for (let i = -gridDivs/2; i <= gridDivs/2; i++) {
  const v = i / gridDivs * gridSize;
  gridPts.push(new THREE.Vector3(-gridSize/2, 0, v));
  gridPts.push(new THREE.Vector3(gridSize/2, 0, v));
  gridPts.push(new THREE.Vector3(v, 0, -gridSize/2));
  gridPts.push(new THREE.Vector3(v, 0, gridSize/2));
}
gridGeo.setFromPoints(gridPts);
const gridMat = new THREE.LineBasicMaterial({ color: 0x374151, transparent: true, opacity: 0.25 });
const customGrid = new THREE.LineSegments(gridGeo, gridMat);
scene.add(customGrid);

const segX = 150, segY = 150;
const surfGeo = new THREE.PlaneGeometry(30, 30, segX, segY);
const surfOrig = [];
const pos0 = surfGeo.attributes.position;
for (let i = 0; i < pos0.count; i++) surfOrig.push({ x: pos0.getX(i), y: pos0.getY(i) });

const surfMat = new THREE.MeshPhongMaterial({ color: 0x1e3a5f, side: THREE.DoubleSide, transparent: true, opacity: 0.65, shininess: 60, flatShading: false });
const surfMesh = new THREE.Mesh(surfGeo, surfMat);
scene.add(surfMesh);

const wireGeo = surfGeo.clone();
const wireOrig = [];
const wpos0 = wireGeo.attributes.position;
for (let i = 0; i < wpos0.count; i++) wireOrig.push({ x: wpos0.getX(i), y: wpos0.getY(i) });
const wireMat = new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ color: 0x60a5fa, wireframe: true, transparent: true, opacity: 0.15 });
const wireMesh = new THREE.Mesh(wireGeo, wireMat);
scene.add(wireMesh);

const planeGeo = new THREE.PlaneGeometry(20, 20);
const planeMat = new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ color: 0x000000, side: THREE.DoubleSide, transparent: true, opacity: 0.55 });
const cutPlane = new THREE.Mesh(planeGeo, planeMat);
scene.add(cutPlane);

const sectionMat = new THREE.LineBasicMaterial({ color: 0xfbbf24, linewidth: 3 });
let sectionLines = [];

function updateSurface() {
  const pos = surfGeo.attributes.position;
  for (let i = 0; i < pos.count; i++) {
    const tx = surfOrig[i].x, ty = surfOrig[i].y;
    const math_z = a*tx*tx*tx + b*tx - ty*ty;
    pos.setXYZ(i, tx, math_z, ty);
  }
  pos.needsUpdate = true; surfGeo.computeVertexNormals();
  const wpos = wireMesh.geometry.attributes.position;
  for (let i = 0; i < wpos.count; i++) {
    const tx = wireOrig[i].x, ty = wireOrig[i].y;
    const math_z = a*tx*tx*tx + b*tx - ty*ty;
    wpos.setXYZ(i, tx, math_z, ty);
  }
  wpos.needsUpdate = true; wireMesh.geometry.computeVertexNormals();
}

function updateSection3D() {
  const th = theta * Math.PI / 180;
  const cosT = Math.cos(th);
  const sinT = Math.sin(th);
  cutPlane.rotation.set(Math.PI / 2, 0, th);
  cutPlane.position.set(-zVal * sinT, zVal * cosT, 0);
  sectionLines.forEach(l => scene.remove(l));
  sectionLines = [];
  const intervals = getIntervals(a, b, zVal, theta);
  if (intervals.length === 1 && intervals[0].vertical) {
    const xFixed = intervals[0].left;
    const pts = [];
    const yMax = 15, segSteps = 200;
    for (let i = 0; i <= segSteps; i++) {
      const y = -yMax + (2 * yMax * i) / segSteps;
      const z = a*xFixed*xFixed*xFixed + b*xFixed - y*y;
      pts.push(new THREE.Vector3(xFixed, z, y));
    }
    const geo = new THREE.BufferGeometry().setFromPoints(pts);
    const line = new THREE.Line(geo, sectionMat);
    scene.add(line); sectionLines.push(line);
    return;
  }
  intervals.forEach(({ left, right }) => {
    const segSteps = Math.max(2, Math.ceil((right - left) / (xMax - xMin) * 600));
    const ddx = (right - left) / segSteps;
    const ptsUpper = [], ptsLower = [];
    for (let i = 0; i <= segSteps; i++) {
      const x = left + i * ddx;
      const fx = a*x*x*x + b*x - x*Math.tan(th) - zVal/cosT;
      const y = Math.sqrt(Math.max(0, fx));
      const z = a*x*x*x + b*x - y*y;
      ptsUpper.push(new THREE.Vector3(x, z, y));
      ptsLower.push(new THREE.Vector3(x, z, -y));
    }
    const leftIsRoot = Math.abs(left - xMin) > 0.001;
    const rightIsRoot = Math.abs(right - xMax) > 0.001;
    const isClosed = leftIsRoot && rightIsRoot;
    if (isClosed) {
      const pts = [...ptsUpper, ...ptsLower.reverse()];
      if (pts.length > 2) {
        const geo = new THREE.BufferGeometry().setFromPoints(pts);
        const line = new THREE.Line(geo, sectionMat);
        scene.add(line); sectionLines.push(line);
      }
    } else {
      if (ptsUpper.length > 1) {
        const geo = new THREE.BufferGeometry().setFromPoints(ptsUpper);
        const line = new THREE.Line(geo, sectionMat);
        scene.add(line); sectionLines.push(line);
      }
      if (ptsLower.length > 1) {
        const geo = new THREE.BufferGeometry().setFromPoints(ptsLower);
        const line = new THREE.Line(geo, sectionMat);
        scene.add(line); sectionLines.push(line);
      }
    }
  });
}

function resize3D() {
  const rect = container3d.getBoundingClientRect();
  camera.aspect = rect.width / rect.height;
  camera.updateProjectionMatrix();
  renderer.setSize(rect.width, rect.height, false);
}

function animate3D() {
  requestAnimationFrame(animate3D);
  controls.update();
  renderer.render(scene, camera);
}

function setupPanel(headerId, panelId) {
  const header = document.getElementById(headerId);
  const panel = document.getElementById(panelId);
  header.addEventListener("click", () => {
    panel.classList.toggle("collapsed");
    setTimeout(() => { resize2D(); resize3D(); }, 350);
  });
}
setupPanel("header-3d", "panel-3d");
setupPanel("header-2d", "panel-2d");

function updateFormula() {
  const sa = a.toFixed(2);
  const sb = b >= 0 ? "+ " + b.toFixed(2) : "- " + Math.abs(b).toFixed(2);
  const sz = zVal >= 0 ? "+ " + zVal.toFixed(2) : "- " + Math.abs(zVal).toFixed(2);
  formulaHint.textContent = `— y² = ${sa}x³ ${sb}x ${sz}`;
  const hint3d = document.getElementById("hint-3d");
  if (hint3d) {
    const sa3 = a.toFixed(2);
    const sb3 = b >= 0 ? "+ " + b.toFixed(2) : "- " + Math.abs(b).toFixed(2);
    hint3d.textContent = `— z = ${sa3}x³ ${sb3}x - y²`;
  }
}

function updateSliderConfig() {
  if (activeParam === "a") {
    slider.min = -10; slider.max = 20; slider.step = 0.01; slider.value = a;
    paramVal.textContent = a.toFixed(2);
  } else if (activeParam === "b") {
    slider.min = -10; slider.max = 10; slider.step = 0.1; slider.value = b;
    paramVal.textContent = b.toFixed(2);
  } else {
    slider.min = -20; slider.max = 20; slider.step = 0.001; slider.value = zVal;
    paramVal.textContent = zVal.toFixed(2);
  }
  updateFormula();
}

function updateZ(val) {
  zVal = Math.max(-20, Math.min(20, parseFloat(val) || 0));
  draw2D(); updateSection3D(); updateFormula();
  if (activeParam === "z") updateSliderConfig();
}

function updateA() {
  a = Math.max(-10, Math.min(20, a));
  draw2D(); updateSurface(); updateSection3D();
  if (activeParam === "a") updateSliderConfig(); else updateFormula();
}

function updateB() {
  b = Math.max(-10, Math.min(10, b));
  draw2D(); updateSurface(); updateSection3D();
  if (activeParam === "b") updateSliderConfig(); else updateFormula();
}

slider.addEventListener("input", () => {
  const val = parseFloat(slider.value);
  if (activeParam === "a") { a = val; updateA(); }
  else if (activeParam === "b") { b = val; updateB(); }
  else { updateZ(val); }
  updateSliderConfig();
});

function setActiveParam(val) {
  activeParam = val;
  document.querySelectorAll(".radio-label").forEach(l => l.classList.remove("active"));
  document.querySelectorAll('input[name="param"]').forEach(r => {
    r.checked = (r.value === val);
    if (r.value === val) r.parentElement.classList.add("active");
  });
  updateSliderConfig();
}

document.querySelectorAll('input[name="param"]').forEach(radio => {
  radio.addEventListener("change", () => setActiveParam(radio.value));
});
document.querySelectorAll(".radio-label").forEach(label => {
  label.addEventListener("click", (e) => {
    if (e.target.tagName === "INPUT") return;
    setActiveParam(label.dataset.value);
  });
});

btnPlay.addEventListener("click", () => {
  if (isPlaying) {
    isPlaying = false; btnPlay.textContent = "▶ Play"; btnPlay.style.background = "#2563eb";
    cancelAnimationFrame(animId);
  } else {
    isPlaying = true; btnPlay.textContent = "⏸ Pause"; btnPlay.style.background = "#ea580c";
    lastTime = 0;
    if (zVal <= -20 && playDir === 1) { playDir = -1; btnDir.textContent = "↑ Up"; }
    if (zVal >= 20 && playDir === -1) { playDir = 1; btnDir.textContent = "↓ Down"; }
    animId = requestAnimationFrame(playLoop);
  }
});

function playLoop(timestamp) {
  if (!isPlaying) return;
  if (!lastTime) lastTime = timestamp;
  const dt = (timestamp - lastTime) / 1000;
  lastTime = timestamp;
  const speed = 2.5;
  let newZ = zVal - playDir * speed * dt;
  if (playDir === 1 && newZ <= -20) { newZ = -20; isPlaying = false; btnPlay.textContent = "▶ Play"; btnPlay.style.background = "#2563eb"; }
  if (playDir === -1 && newZ >= 20) { newZ = 20; isPlaying = false; btnPlay.textContent = "▶ Play"; btnPlay.style.background = "#2563eb"; }
  updateZ(newZ);
  if (isPlaying) animId = requestAnimationFrame(playLoop);
}

btnDir.addEventListener("click", () => {
  playDir = playDir === 1 ? -1 : 1;
  btnDir.textContent = playDir === 1 ? "↓ Down" : "↑ Up";
});

thetaSlider.addEventListener("input", () => {
  theta = parseFloat(thetaSlider.value);
  thetaVal.textContent = theta.toFixed(1) + "°";
  draw2D(); updateSection3D(); updateFormula();
});

setupClickIncrement(paramVal,
  () => { if (activeParam === "a") return a; if (activeParam === "b") return b; return zVal; },
  (v) => {
    if (activeParam === "a") { a = v; updateA(); }
    else if (activeParam === "b") { b = v; updateB(); }
    else { updateZ(v); }
    updateSliderConfig();
  },
  0.01,
  () => activeParam === "a" ? -10 : (activeParam === "b" ? -10 : -20),
  () => activeParam === "a" ? 20 : (activeParam === "b" ? 10 : 20)
);

setupClickIncrement(thetaVal,
  () => theta,
  (v) => {
    theta = Math.max(0, Math.min(180, v));
    thetaSlider.value = theta;
    thetaVal.textContent = theta.toFixed(1) + "°";
    draw2D(); updateSection3D(); updateFormula();
  },
  0.1,
  () => 0, () => 180
);

setTimeout(() => {
  resize2D(); resize3D();
  updateSurface(); updateSection3D();
  animate3D(); updateSliderConfig(); updateFormula();
  thetaVal.textContent = theta.toFixed(1) + "°";
}, 100);

window.addEventListener("resize", () => { resize2D(); resize3D(); });
</script>
</body>
</html>
```

## Kimi

处理无穷点绘制文本框与虚线

用户反馈了一个具体的ECC动画问题：

- 私钥400的二进制是110010000（从高位开始）
- 当前Step 1/12: Double (bit 0) 和 Step 2/12: Add (bit 0) 的文字提示都正确
- 但画面上没有看到相应的切线（tangent line）和连线（chord line）

看截图：

- 图1（Step 1/12）：画面上只有G点，没有切线
- 图2（Step 2/12）：画面上有一个白圈蓝点（当前点），但没有连线

分析代码中的 `drawECCOverlay`：

JavaScript 复制

```javascript
// Draw all historical steps (faint gray)
const histSteps = Math.min(ECC.currentStep, ECC.steps.length);
for (let i = 0; i < histSteps; i++) {
    const step = ECC.steps[i];
    if (!step.from.isInfinity && step.line) {
      drawLineStd(step.line, "#9ca3af", 0.5, [3, 4]);
    }
    ...
}

// Draw current step highlight (brighter)
if (ECC.currentStep > 0 && ECC.currentStep <= ECC.steps.length) {
    const cur = ECC.steps[ECC.currentStep - 1];
    if (cur && !cur.from.isInfinity && cur.line) {
      const lineColor = cur.type === 'double' ? "#f87171" : "#60a5fa";
      drawLineStd(cur.line, lineColor, 1.2, [4, 3]);
    }
    ...
}
```

问题可能出在：

1. `step.line` 为 null
2. `drawLineStd` 函数计算出的点不在可视范围内
3. 无穷远点相关的逻辑问题

让我检查 `getTangentLine` 和 `getChordLine`：

JavaScript 复制

```javascript
function getTangentLine(P) {
  if (P.isInfinity) return null;
  const lam = (3 * P.X * P.X + A_std) / (2 * P.Y);
  const c = P.Y - lam * P.X;
  return { lam, c };
}
```

对于 Step 1 (Double)，起始点是从无穷远点开始的。在 `precomputeSteps` 中：

JavaScript 复制

```javascript
let R = { ...startStd }; // startStd = { isInfinity: true }
for (let i = 0; i < bits.length; i++) {
    const lineD = getTangentLine(R); // R is infinity -> returns null
    const R2 = ecDouble(R); // returns { isInfinity: true }
    ...
    steps.push({
      type: 'double', ...
      line: lineD, // null
      intersection: intD, // null
    });
    R = { ...R2 }; // still infinity
    if (bits[i] === '1') {
      const lineA = getChordLine(R, baseStd); // R is infinity -> returns null
      ...
    }
}
```

啊！问题在这里。当从无穷远点开始计算时，第一步的 `from` 是无穷远点，所以 `getTangentLine` 返回 null，导致没有切线画出。但实际上，在椭圆曲线标量乘法中，从无穷远点开始，第一步应该是：

- 2 * ∞ = ∞ (double)
- ∞ + G = G (add)

这两个操作在几何上确实没有直观的切线/弦线，因为它们涉及无穷远点。但是，用户期望看到动画。对于第一个"1"位（最高位），在标准的double-and-add算法中，通常的处理是：

- 初始值设为无穷远
- 对每一位：
  
  - 当前值 = 2 * 当前值
  - 如果位是1：当前值 = 当前值 + G

对于最高位是1的情况：

- Step 1: Double ∞ -> ∞ （无几何意义）
- Step 2: Add ∞ + G -> G （几何上就是从G画一条垂直线交于第三点，然后反射...但∞ + G = G，没有弦线）

所以前两个步骤确实没有可画的几何线，因为涉及无穷远点。但是，这会让用户困惑。更好的做法是：

1. 对于涉及无穷远点的步骤，在画面上显示文字说明（已经在做了：`∞ · 2 = ∞` 和 `∞ + G = G`）
2. 或者，跳过这些无几何意义的步骤，直接从第一个有实际几何意义的步骤开始显示

但用户明确说"画面为什么没有相应的切线和连线表示这两个操作呢"。让我再仔细看截图。图1 Step 1/12 画面上只有G点。图2 Step 2/12 画面上有一个白圈蓝点（看起来是在G点附近？）。等等，图2中的白圈蓝点看起来在G点的左侧下方。这可能是 Step 2 的 `to` 点？但 `∞ + G = G`，所以 `to` 点应该是G本身。让我再检查 `ecAdd`：

对于 Step 2 (Add infinity + G): 确实没有弦线，因为其中一个点是无穷远点。但是，用户可能不理解为什么没线。我应该：

```javascript
function drawLineStd(line, color, width, dash) {

  }
}
```

2. 对历史步骤中 `from.isInfinity` 的，也显示提示（或者跳过不画线）
3. 确保当前步骤高亮即使与已有标签重合也可见

另外，我发现 `drawECCOverlay` 中当前步骤的高亮逻辑有一个小问题：当 `cur.from.isInfinity` 时，不会画高亮线（因为 `!cur.from.isInfinity` 条件），但会画高亮点（如果 `!cur.to.isInfinity`）。这是对的。但用户看不到线，所以觉得"没有相应表示"。我需要在无穷远步骤时画一些替代视觉元素。修改计划：

- 在 `drawECCOverlay` 中，处理当前步骤时，如果 `from.isInfinity`：
  
  - 在G点旁边画一个文字框，写 "2·∞ = ∞" 或 "∞ + G = G"
  - 画一条从文字框到G点的虚线
- 同时，确保这些步骤在底部提示中也有说明（已经有了）

让我写代码。

这是正常的——前两个步骤涉及**无穷远点**（Identity），在椭圆曲线几何中确实**没有可画的切线或弦线**：

- **Step 1** `2·∞ = ∞`：无穷远点没有切线
- **Step 2** `∞ + G = G`：其中一个点是无穷远，没有弦线

但这样会让用户困惑，所以我给涉及无穷远点的步骤加了**文字气泡 + 指示虚线**，让你明确知道"这一步是合法的，只是没有几何线"。同时让当前步骤的高亮终点更明显，即使和 `G` 重合也能看到。下面是修复后的完整文件：

HTML 预览 复制

```html
<!DOCTYPE html>

  cursor: pointer; font-weight: 600; white-space: nowrap; }

  border-bottom: 2px solid #1f2937; transition: flex 0.35s cubic-bezier(.4,0,.2,1); position: relative; }

  padding: 6px 14px; background: #0f172a; border-bottom: 1px solid #1f2937;
  cursor: pointer; user-select: none; }

#three-container { width: 100%; height: 100%; }

.ecc-step-hint {
```
